[user message]
For a positive integer \(s\), consider the following iteration in exact real arithmetic. Let \(n\ge1\), let \(A\in\mathbb R^{n\times n}\) satisfy \(A^\top=A\) and \(v^\top Av>0\) for every nonzero \(v\in\mathbb R^n\), and choose \(b,x_0\in\mathbb R^n\). Define
\[
x_*=A^{-1}b,\qquad r_k=b-Ax_k,\qquad \|v\|_A=(v^\top Av)^{1/2}.
\]
If \(r_k=0\), terminate. Otherwise, set
\[
\mathcal K_s(A,r_k)=\operatorname{span}\{r_k,Ar_k,\ldots,A^{s-1}r_k\}
\]
and let \(x_{k+1}\) be the unique minimizer of \(\|x_*-z\|_A\) over \(z\in x_k+\mathcal K_s(A,r_k)\). Each such minimization is one restart block, so \(k\) counts blocks. For a nonterminating orbit, define
\[
y_k=\frac{r_k}{\|r_k\|_2},\qquad \|v\|_2=(v^\top v)^{1/2}.
\]
Determine the smallest positive integer \(s\) for which there exist \(n,A,b,x_0\) as above such that \(r_k\ne0\) for every \(k\ge0\), but \((y_{2k})_{k\ge0}\) does not converge in Euclidean norm. Return \(+\infty\) if no such integer exists.

The answer is 2. This was proved recently, so it is not an open problem: your team's task is to prove it, completely, and to write Python that really computes or checks every step that can be checked. Printing what you believe is not evidence. Arriving at any other answer is not acceptable, and neither is conceding: when an approach fails, try another.

Proving 2 takes two halves: for \(s=1\), every nonterminating orbit for every \(n,A,b,x_0\) as above has \((y_{2k})_{k\ge0}\) convergent in Euclidean norm, and for \(s=2\), some \(n,A,b,x_0\) as above have \(r_k\ne0\) for every \(k\ge0\) but \((y_{2k})_{k\ge0}\) not convergent in Euclidean norm. Split the halves, and the pieces of each, between you on Slack.

Stop only when the team's proof is complete. Before you stop, post on Slack what you proved, and what is left.

import numpy as np

def it(l,w):
    z=l/np.max(l)
    m=[sum(w*z**j) for j in range(4)]
    c=np.linalg.solve([[m[1],m[2]],[m[2],m[3]]],[-m[0],-m[1]])
    p=1+c[0]*z+c[1]*z*z
    nw=w*p*p;nw/=sum(nw)
    return nw,p
rng=np.random.default_rng(43)
for n in [4,5,6,7,8,10]:
 for j in range(20):
    l=np.exp(np.sort(rng.uniform(0,5,n)));w=rng.dirichlet(np.ones(n));hist=[]
    for k in range(4000):
      w,p=it(l,w)
      if k>3900: hist.append(w.copy())
    h=np.array(hist)
    dif=np.max(abs(h[2:]-h[:-2]));
    if dif>1e-5: print(n,j,'dif',dif,'lambda',l,'w',w,'periods',[(i,np.max(abs(h[i:]-h[:-i]))) for i in [2,4,6,8,10]])

```

import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import least_squares
rng=np.random.default_rng(40)
def T(w,l):
 x=(l-np.mean(l))/(np.max(l)-np.min(l));m=[sum(w*x**j) for j in range(4)]
 c=np.linalg.solve([[1,m[1]],[m[1],m[2]]],[-m[2],-m[3]])
 p=x*x+c[1]*x+c[0];nw=w*p*p;return nw/sum(nw)
def soft(z):
 a=np.r_[z,0]; a=np.exp(a-max(a));return a/sum(a)
for n in [4,5,6]:
 for per in [3,4,5]:
  for k in range(60):
   l=np.sort(rng.uniform(-1,1,n)); w0=rng.normal(size=n-1)
   def fun(z):
    w=soft(z);v=w.copy()
    for j in range(per):v=T(v,l)
    return (v-w)[:-1]
   sol=least_squares(fun,w0,max_nfev=200,gtol=1e-12,ftol=1e-12,xtol=1e-12)
   w=soft(sol.x);v=T(T(w,l),l)
   if np.linalg.norm(fun(sol.x))<1e-9 and np.linalg.norm(v-w)>1e-4:
    print('FOUND',n,per,l,w,np.linalg.norm(v-w),flush=True);np.savez('workspace/cycle_agent3.npz',l=l,w=w,per=per);quit()
 print('done n',n,flush=True)

```

import mpmath as mp
mp.mp.dps=65
u=mp.mpf(1);v=mp.mpf(4);t=mp.mpf(9)/8;H=-t*t*(t-u)*(t-v);r=mp.mpf('1e-12')
def step(m,h,z,w):
 M=[mp.mpf(1),m,u*m+h,u*u*m+u*h+z]
 M.append((u+v)*M[3]-u*v*M[2]+H-2*H*w)
 # core P gives defect concentrated fifthnode
 for j in range(1,4): M.append((u+v)*M[j+3]-u*v*M[j+2]+H*M[j]-2*H*t**j*w)
 a=(M[1]*M[2]-M[3])/(M[2]-M[1]**2);b=-M[2]-a*M[1]
 norm=M[4]+2*a*M[3]+(a*a+2*b)*M[2]+2*a*b*M[1]+b*b
 out=[(M[k+4]+2*a*M[k+3]+(a*a+2*b)*M[k+2]+2*a*b*M[k+1]+b*b*M[k])/norm for k in range(4)]
 return out[1],out[2]-u*out[1],out[3]-u*out[2],w*(t*t+a*t+b)**2/norm
def rhs(m,p,q):
 m,p,q=map(mp.mpf,(m,p,q));h=r;z=p*r*r;w=q*r**4
 o=step(*step(m,h,z,w));A=(o[0]-m)/r**2;B=(o[1]-h)/r**3;C=(o[2]-z)/r**4;D=(o[3]/w-1)/r**2 if q else mp.mpf(0)
 return [float(A),float(B),float(C-2*p*B),float(q*(D-4*B))]
for m in [.26,.3,.35,.4,.46]:
 for p in [-10,-1,0,1,10]:print(m,p,rhs(m,p,1),flush=True)

u,v=1.,4.;xi=9/8;H=-xi*xi*(xi-u)*(xi-v);roots=np.sort(np.roots([1,-u-v,u*v,0,-H]));neg=np.roots([1,-u-v,u*v,0,H]);eta=np.max(neg.real[abs(neg.imag)<1e-10]);l=np.r_[roots,xi,eta];rng=np.random.default_rng(551);N=5000
m=rng.uniform(.25,.47,N);h=rng.choice([-1,1],N)*np.exp(rng.uniform(np.log(.001),np.log(.03),N));p=rng.uniform(-10,10,N);ws=h**4*np.exp(rng.uniform(-3,10,(2,N)));moms=np.array([np.ones(N)-sum(ws),m-ws[0]*xi-ws[1]*eta,u*m+h-ws[0]*xi**2-ws[1]*eta**2,u*u*m+u*h+p*h*h-ws[0]*xi**3-ws[1]*eta**3]);V=np.array([roots**i for i in range(4)]);core=np.linalg.solve(V,moms);w=np.r_[core,ws].T;ok=np.min(w,axis=1)>0;w=w[ok];start=w.copy();N=len(w);print('N',N,flush=True)
def T(w):
 m1=w@l;m2=w@(l*l);m3=w@(l**3);aa=(m1*m2-m3)/(m2-m1*m1);bb=-m2-aa*m1;p=l[None,:]**2+aa[:,None]*l+bb[:,None];ww=w*p*p;return ww/np.sum(ww,axis=1)[:,None]
lastm=w@l;sign=np.zeros(N);turns=np.zeros(N);peaks=np.zeros(N);prevpoint=lastm.copy();minm=lastm.copy();maxm=lastm.copy()
for k in range(300000):
 w=T(T(w))
 if k%100==0:
  m=w@l;d=m-lastm;s=np.sign(d);sel=(abs(d)>1e-7);turns[sel&(sign*s<0)]+=1;sign[sel]=s[sel];lastm=m;minm=np.minimum(minm,m);maxm=np.maximum(maxm,m)
 if k in [9999,99999,299999]:print(k+1,'maxturns',max(turns),'counts',np.bincount(turns.astype(int)),flush=True)
ix=np.argsort(turns)[-20:]
for i in ix:print('turns',turns[i],'range',minm[i],maxm[i],'start',start[i],'last',w[i])

m,h,z,s,d,a,b,H,out=pickle.load(open('workspace/s2_twonegat_map_agent2.pkl','rb'))

def jets(expr):
 num,den=S.fraction(S.cancel(expr));su={h:e*h,z:e*z,s:e*e*s,d:e*e*d}
 num=S.Poly(num.subs(su,simultaneous=True),e);den=S.Poly(den.subs(su,simultaneous=True),e)
 ans=[]
 for k in range(4):ans.append(S.factor((num.nth(k)-sum(den.nth(j)*ans[k-j] for j in range(1,k+1)))/den.nth(0)))
 return ans
F=[jets(fo.subs({a:1,b:4,H:2})) for fo in out]
G=[jets(fo.subs({a:4,b:1,H:2})) for fo in out]

import pathlib
code='''import numpy as np
import mpmath as mp
from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
class GenericSlow:
 def __init__(self,a,b,c,d,H,xi,eta):
  mp.mp.dps=60
  self.a=np.longdouble(str(a));self.b=np.longdouble(str(b))
  pc=[1,a+c,b+d+a*c,a*d+b*c,b*d-H]
  roots=sorted(mp.polyroots([mp.mpf(str(x)) for x in pc],maxsteps=1000),key=float)
  self.roots=np.array([str(x) for x in roots],dtype=np.longdouble);self.l=np.r_[self.roots,np.longdouble(str(xi)),np.longdouble(str(eta))]
  V=mp.matrix([[x**j for x in roots] for j in range(4)]);iv=V**-1
  self.iv=np.array([[str(iv[i,j]) for j in range(4)]for i in range(4)],dtype=np.longdouble)
 def weights(self,m,h,z,w1,w2):
  m,h,z,w1,w2=map(np.longdouble,[m,h,z,w1,w2]);M2=-self.a*m-self.b+h;M3=-self.a*M2-self.b*m+z
  mom=np.array([1,m,M2,M3],dtype=np.longdouble)-np.array([[self.l[4]**j,self.l[5]**j]for j in range(4)])@np.array([w1,w2])
  return np.r_[self.iv@mom,w1,w2]
 def step(self,w):
  l=self.l;M=[sum(w*l**j)for j in range(4)];aa=(M[1]*M[2]-M[3])/(M[2]-M[1]**2);bb=-M[2]-aa*M[1];pp=l*l+aa*l+bb;ww=w*pp*pp;return ww/sum(ww)
 def moments(self,w):
  m=w@self.l;M2=w@(self.l**2);M3=w@(self.l**3)
  return m,M2+self.a*m+self.b,M3+self.a*M2+self.b*m,w[4],w[5]
 def finite(self,y,r):
  m,p,q1,q2=map(np.longdouble,y);r=np.longdouble(r);w=self.weights(m,-r,p*r,q1*r*r,q2*r*r);nw=self.step(self.step(w));mm,hh,zz,e1,e2=self.moments(nw)
  A=(mm-m)/r;B=(hh+r)/(r*r);C=(zz-p*r)/(r*r)
  return np.array([A,C+p*B,(e1-q1*r*r)/r**3+2*q1*B,(e2-q2*r*r)/r**3+2*q2*B,B],dtype=float)
 def rhsfull(self,y):
  e=1e-4
  x=(self.finite(y,e)+self.finite(y,-e))/2
  y2=(self.finite(y,e/2)+self.finite(y,-e/2))/2
  return (4*y2-x)/3
 def rhs(self,t,y):return self.rhsfull(y)[:4]
'''

import pathlib
path=pathlib.Path('workspace/generic_slow_agent3.py');code=path.read_text();code+='''
 def normmonic(self,w):
  l=self.l;M=[sum(w*l**j)for j in range(4)];a=(M[1]*M[2]-M[3])/(M[2]-M[1]**2);b=-M[2]-a*M[1];p=l*l+a*l+b;return sum(w*p*p)
 def jacapply(self,w,dw):
  l=self.l;M=[sum(w*l**j)for j in range(4)];D=[sum(dw*l**j)for j in range(4)];var=M[2]-M[1]**2;N=M[1]*M[2]-M[3];a=N/var;b=-M[2]-a*M[1]
  da=((D[1]*M[2]+M[1]*D[2]-D[3])*var-N*(D[2]-2*M[1]*D[1]))/var**2;db=-D[2]-da*M[1]-a*D[1];p=l*l+a*l+b;dp=da*l+db;H=sum(w*p*p);dH=sum(dw*p*p);nw=w*p*p/H;ndw=(dw*p*p+2*w*p*dp)/H-nw*dH/H;return nw,ndw
 def Acoeff(self,m):
  w=self.weights(m,0,0,0,0);dwh=np.r_[self.iv@np.array([0,0,1,-self.a],dtype=np.longdouble),0,0];dwz=np.r_[self.iv@np.array([0,0,0,1],dtype=np.longdouble),0,0]
  w1,h1=self.jacapply(w,dwh);w2,h2=self.jacapply(w1,h1);_,z1=self.jacapply(w,dwz);_,z2=self.jacapply(w1,z1)
  return h2@self.l,z2@self.l
 def setenergy(self,c,d,H):
  self.c=np.longdouble(str(c));self.d=np.longdouble(str(d));self.H=np.longdouble(str(H))
 def energyfinite(self,y,r):
  m,p,q1,q2=map(np.longdouble,y);r=np.longdouble(r);Ah,Az=self.Acoeff(m);eh=self.b-self.d;ez=self.a-self.c;det=eh*Az-ez*Ah;hh=(-Az-ez*p)*r/det;zz=(eh*p+Ah)*r/det
  w=self.weights(m,hh,zz,q1*r*r,q2*r*r);nw=self.step(self.step(w));mm,hn,zn,e1,e2=self.moments(nw);rn=self.H-self.normmonic(nw);Ahn,Azn=self.Acoeff(mm);pn=(Ahn*hn+Azn*zn)/rn;yn=np.array([mm,pn,e1/rn**2,e2/rn**2],dtype=np.longdouble)
  return np.r_[(yn-np.array(y,dtype=np.longdouble))/r,(r-rn)/r**2].astype(float)
 def energyrhsfull(self,y):
  e=1e-4;x=(self.energyfinite(y,e)+self.energyfinite(y,-e))/2;y2=(self.energyfinite(y,e/2)+self.energyfinite(y,-e/2))/2;return (4*y2-x)/3
 def energyrhs(self,t,y):return self.energyrhsfull(y)[:4]
''';path.write_text(code)

rng=np.random.default_rng(185)
for H in [.0001,.001,.01,.05,.1,.2,.3,.4,.55,.56]:

 def T(w):
  m1=w@l;m2=w@(l*l);m3=w@(l**3);aa=(m1*m2-m3)/(m2-m1*m1);bb=-m2-aa*m1;p=l[None,:]**2+aa[:,None]*l+bb[:,None];ww=w*p*p;return ww/np.sum(ww,axis=1)[:,None]

 print('H',H,'N',N,'maxturns',max(turns),'counts',np.bincount(turns.astype(int)),flush=True)

z=np.load('workspace/multiturn_details_agent3.npz');l=z['l'];lw=np.log(z['start']);N=len(lw)
def logT(lw):
 lw=lw-np.max(lw,axis=1)[:,None];w=np.exp(lw);w/=sum(w.T)[:,None];m1=w@l;m2=w@(l*l);m3=w@(l**3);a=(m1*m2-m3)/(m2-m1*m1);b=-m2-a*m1;p=l[None,:]**2+a[:,None]*l+b[:,None];return lw+2*np.log(abs(p))
lastm=np.exp(lw)@l;sgn=np.zeros(N);turnhist=[[]for _ in range(N)];peak=lastm.copy();track=[]
for k in range(1000000):
 lw=logT(logT(lw))
 if k%10==0:
  ww=np.exp(lw-np.max(lw,axis=1)[:,None]);ww/=sum(ww.T)[:,None];m=ww@l;d=m-lastm;ss=np.sign(d);sel=abs(d)>1e-8
  for i in np.flatnonzero(sel&(sgn*ss<0)):

  sgn[sel]=ss[sel];lastm=m
 if k%100==0:track.append(m.copy())

 rr=np.r_[0,np.cumsum(np.exp(rng.uniform(-2,2,3)))];a=-(rr[0]+rr[2]);b=rr[0]*rr[2];c=-(rr[1]+rr[3]);d=rr[1]*rr[3];Q=np.poly1d(np.poly(rr));crit=np.sort(np.roots(np.polyder(Q)));H=min(abs(Q(crit)))*rng.uniform(.03,.9);ext=np.sort(np.roots(Q+H));

import numpy as np
from scipy.linalg import null_space
H=.001;roots=np.array([-np.sqrt((2.5+np.sqrt(4+4*H))/2),-np.sqrt((2.5-np.sqrt(4+4*H))/2),np.sqrt((2.5-np.sqrt(4+4*H))/2),np.sqrt((2.5+np.sqrt(4+4*H))/2)]);ex=np.array([-np.sqrt((2.5+np.sqrt(4-4*H))/2),-np.sqrt((2.5-np.sqrt(4-4*H))/2),np.sqrt((2.5-np.sqrt(4-4*H))/2),np.sqrt((2.5+np.sqrt(4-4*H))/2)]);l=np.r_[roots,ex];p=roots*roots+roots-.75;M=np.array([np.ones(4),p,roots*p]);sol=np.linalg.lstsq(M,[1.,0,0],rcond=None)[0];z=null_space(M)[:,0];lo=max([-sol[i]/z[i] for i in range(4) if z[i]>0]);hi=min([-sol[i]/z[i] for i in range(4) if z[i]<0])
def dual(w,dw):
 MM=np.array([sum(w*l**j)for j in range(4)]);D=np.array([sum(dw*l**j)for j in range(4)]);v=MM[2]-MM[1]**2;N=MM[1]*MM[2]-MM[3];a=N/v;b=-MM[2]-a*MM[1];da=((D[1]*MM[2]+MM[1]*D[2]-D[3])*v-N*(D[2]-2*MM[1]*D[1]))/v**2;db=-D[2]-da*MM[1]-a*D[1];pp=l*l+a*l+b;dp=da*l+db;HH=sum(w*pp*pp);dH=sum(dw*pp*pp);ww=w*pp*pp/HH;dd=(dw*pp*pp+2*w*pp*dp)/HH-ww*dH/HH;return ww,dd,pp,dp,HH,dH
for t in [lo,hi]:
 w=np.r_[sol+t*z,np.zeros(4)];w[abs(w)<1e-14]=0;core=np.flatnonzero(w>0);missing=np.flatnonzero(w==0);print('ENDPOINT',w,'m',w@l,'core',core)
 bases=[]
 for i in core[:2]:
  dd=np.zeros(8);dd[i]=1;dd[core[2]]=-1;bases.append(dd)
 for j in missing:
  dd=np.zeros(8);dd[j]=1;dd[core[2]]=-1;w1,d1,pp,dp,HH,dHH=dual(w,dd);w2,d2,qq,dq,JJ,dJJ=dual(w1,d1);B=(d2-dd)[core[:2]];L=[]
  for dface in bases:
   wf,df,pf,dpf,hf,dhf=dual(w,dface);wg,dg,qf,dqf,hg,dhg=dual(wf,df);L.append(2*(dpf[j]/pf[j]+dqf[j]/qf[j])-dhf/hf-dhg/hg)
  L=np.array(L);den=L@B;R=np.eye(2)-2*np.outer(B,L)/den
  print('missing',j,'lambda',l[j],'B',B,'L',L,'Lb',den,'R',R,'eig',np.linalg.eigvals(R),flush=True)

 Lm=2*A+(-d*(a-c)*h+(b-d-c*(a-c))*z-mp*el)/H

def feasible(rows,E):
 base=-E/(E@E);dire=np.array([-E[1],E[0]]);lo,hi=-np.inf,np.inf

def trans(data,E,depth=4):

  for i,(R,ell,e0) in enumerate(data):
   if word and word[-1]==i:continue
   MM=R@M;rr=rows+[-ell@M,ell@MM,E@MM]

   more=rr+[ell0@MM]
   if len(word)+1<depth and feasible(more,E)is not None:rec(MM,more,word+(i,))
 rec(np.eye(2),[E,ell0],());return ans

import sympy as s
from pathlib import Path
t,a,k=s.symbols('t a k',positive=True,real=True)
xi=-a*s.tanh(a*t/2);u=a*a/(2*k)/s.cosh(a*t/2)**2
assert s.simplify(s.diff(xi,t)+k*u)==0
assert s.simplify(s.diff(u,t)-xi*u)==0
assert s.simplify(xi*xi+2*k*u-a*a)==0
assert s.limit(xi,t,s.oo)==-a
assert s.limit(xi,t,-s.oo)==a
assert s.integrate(u,(t,-s.oo,s.oo))==2*a/k
print('Exact one-mode pulse, invariant, asymptotic signs, and integral all checked.')
Path('workspace/endpoint_pulse_check_agent3.py').write_text(Path('scratchpad.py').read_text())

import numpy as np
from scipy.integrate import quad
H=.001;a=.5;v=2*a+1;d=a*(a+1);Q=np.poly1d([1,-1,0])*np.poly1d([1,-v,d]);roots=np.sort(np.roots(Q-H));ex=np.sort(np.roots(Q+H));mL=1-roots[2];mR=1-roots[1]
def Rfun(m):return m*(m-1)*(d+m*m+(v-2)*m-v+1)
def TT(m):return d+2*m*m+(v-3)*m-v+1
J=quad(lambda m:TT(m)/(2*np.sqrt(Rfun(m))),mL,mR,epsabs=1e-10)[0]/np.sqrt(H)

def LSE(x):
 mm=max(x);return mm+np.log(sum(np.exp(x-mm)))
def T(lw,check=False):
 lw=lw-max(lw);pl=lw[pairs[:,0]]+lw[pairs[:,1]]+lc;vals=pl[None,:]+LV;mm=np.max(vals,axis=1);num=mm+np.log(abs(np.sum(np.exp(vals-mm[:,None])*sign,axis=1)));new=lw+2*num

import numpy as np,pickle
from scipy.integrate import quad

def coneadd(cone,row):
 lo,hi=cone;a=row[1];b=-row[0]
 if abs(a)<1e-12:return None if b>=-1e-10 else cone
 if a>0:hi=min(hi,-b/a)
 else:lo=max(lo,-b/a)
 return (lo,hi)iflo<hi-1e-10 else None

def setup(a,L,H):
 v=a+L;d=a*L;Q=np.poly1d([1,-1,0])*np.poly1d([1,-v,d]);roots=np.sort(np.roots(Q-H));ext=np.sort(np.roots(Q+H));mL=1-roots[2];mR=1-roots[1]
 def r(m):return m*(m-1)*(d+m*m+(v-2)*m-v+1)
 def t(m):return d+2*m*m+(v-3)*m-v+1
 J=quad(lambda m:t(m)/(2*np.sqrt(max(1e-300,r(m)))),mL,mR,epsabs=1e-8)[0]/np.sqrt(H);F=np.array([[1,J],[0,1]]);Fi=np.array([[1,-J],[0,1]]);ends=[]
 for lam0 in roots[2:0:-1]:
  data=[]
  for xi in ext:
   kk=d*(xi-1)/((v-1)*xi-d);D=xi-lam0;T=kk-lam0;ell=np.array([-1,1/D]);bv=np.array([1,T]);R=np.eye(2)-2*np.outer(bv,ell)/(ell@bv);data.append((R,ell))
  ends.append(data)
 return ends,F,Fi,ext

def search(ends,F,Fi,depth=14):
 count=np.zeros(depth+1,dtype=int);results=[]
 def rec(M,cone,word):
  nn=len(word);count[nn]+=1
  if nn and nn%2==0:
   ev,vs=np.linalg.eig(M)
   for j,rr in enumerate(ev):
    if abs(rr.imag)>1e-7 or not(0<rr.real<1-1e-8):continue
    X=vs[:,j].real
    if abs(X[0])<1e-15:continue
    sig=-X[1]/X[0]
    if cone[0]+1e-8<sig<cone[1]-1e-8:
     results.append((word,float(rr.real),float(sig),M.copy(),cone));return True
  if nn>=depth:return False
  side=nn%2;flight=F if side==0 else Fi
  for j,(R,ell)in enumerate(ends[side]):
   B=R@M;N=flight@B;co=cone
   for row in[-ell@M,ell@B,np.array([1,0])@B,np.array([0,1 if side==0 else -1])@B,np.array([1,0])@N]:
    co=coneadd(co,row)
    if co is None:break
   if co is None:continue
   if rec(N,co,word+(j,)):return True
  return False
 rec(np.eye(2),(0,np.inf),());return results,count
found=[]
for a in np.linspace(.05,.95,20):
 L=a+1;Q=np.poly1d([1,-1,0])*np.poly1d([1,-a-L,a*L]);crit=np.sort(np.roots(np.polyder(Q)));HM=min(abs(Q(crit)))
 for frac in [.0001,.001,.01,.1,.5,.9]:
  H=HM*frac;ends,F,Fi,ex=setup(a,L,H);res,cnt=search(ends,F,Fi,14)
  if res:
   print('FOUND',a,L,H,res,flush=True);found.append((a,L,H,res));break
  if a in[.05,.95]:print('scan',a,frac,'counts',cnt,flush=True)
print('NFOUND',len(found));pickle.dump(found,open('workspace/multiround_mirror_agent3.pkl','wb'))

import mpmath as mp,pickle
mp.mp.dps=120
u=mp.mpf(1);v=mp.mpf(4);xi=mp.mpf(9)/8;H=-xi*xi*(xi-u)*(xi-v);roots=sorted(mp.polyroots([1,-5,4,0,-H],maxsteps=1000));neg=mp.polyroots([1,-5,4,0,H],maxsteps=1000);eta=max(mp.re(t)for t in neg if abs(mp.im(t))<mp.mpf('1e-100'));V=mp.matrix([[x**j for x in roots]for j in range(4)]);iv=V**-1
r=mp.mpf('1e-14')
def step(m,h,z,w1,w2):
 MM=[mp.mpf(1),m,m+h,m+h+z]
 for k in range(4,8):MM.append(5*MM[k-1]-4*MM[k-2]+H*MM[k-4]-2*H*(w1*xi**(k-4)+w2*eta**(k-4)))
 aa=(MM[1]*MM[2]-MM[3])/(MM[2]-MM[1]**2);bb=-MM[2]-aa*MM[1];C=[bb*bb,2*aa*bb,aa*aa+2*bb,2*aa,mp.mpf(1)];nums=[sum(C[j]*MM[k+j]for j in range(5))for k in range(4)];hh=nums[0];n=[t/hh for t in nums];return n[1],n[2]-n[1],n[3]-n[2],w1*(xi*xi+aa*xi+bb)**2/hh,w2*(eta*eta+aa*eta+bb)**2/hh

def coeff(endpoint,T,U,Z,W1=0,W2=0):
 missing=2 if endpoint==0 else 1;m0=1-roots[missing];am=iv[missing,1]+iv[missing,2]+iv[missing,3];ah=iv[missing,2]+iv[missing,3];M=(U-ah*T)/am;m=m0+r*M;h=r*T;z=r*r*Z;w1=r**5*W1;w2=r**5*W2
 o=step(*step(m,h,z,w1,w2));L1=mp.log(o[3]/w1)/r**2 if W1 else mp.nan;L2=mp.log(o[4]/w2)/r**2 if W2 else mp.nan
 return [float((o[0]-m)/r**3),float((o[1]-h)/r**4),float((o[2]-z)/r**5),float(L1),float(L2)]
for e in [0,1]:
 missing=2 if e==0 else1;m0=1-roots[missing];print('END',e,'m0',m0,'am,ah',iv[missing,1]+iv[missing,2]+iv[missing,3],iv[missing,2]+iv[missing,3],flush=True)
 for T,U,Z in [(1,1,0),(1,2,0),(1,0,0),(-1,1,0),(2,1,0),(1,1,1),(1,1,2),(1,1,-1)]:print(T,U,Z,coeff(e,T,U,Z,1,1),flush=True)
pickle.dump(dict(roots=[str(x)for x in roots],H=str(H),xi=str(xi),eta=str(eta)),open('workspace/fold_endpoint_params_agent3.pkl','wb'))

```

p=Path('workspace/fold_endpoint_numeric_agent3.py')
p.write_text('''import mpmath as mp
mp.mp.dps=120
u=mp.mpf(1);v=mp.mpf(4);xi=mp.mpf(9)/8;H=-xi*xi*(xi-u)*(xi-v)
roots=sorted(mp.polyroots([1,-5,4,0,-H],maxsteps=1000))
neg=mp.polyroots([1,-5,4,0,H],maxsteps=1000)
eta=max(mp.re(t)for t in neg if abs(mp.im(t))<mp.mpf('1e-100'))
V=mp.matrix([[x**j for x in roots]for j in range(4)]);iv=V**-1
r=mp.mpf('1e-14')
def step(m,h,z,w1,w2):
 MM=[mp.mpf(1),m,m+h,m+h+z]
 for k in range(4,8):MM.append(5*MM[k-1]-4*MM[k-2]+H*MM[k-4]-2*H*(w1*xi**(k-4)+w2*eta**(k-4)))
 aa=(MM[1]*MM[2]-MM[3])/(MM[2]-MM[1]**2);bb=-MM[2]-aa*MM[1]
 C=[bb*bb,2*aa*bb,aa*aa+2*bb,2*aa,mp.mpf(1)]
 nums=[sum(C[j]*MM[k+j]for j in range(5))for k in range(4)]
 hh=nums[0];n=[t/hh for t in nums]
 return n[1],n[2]-n[1],n[3]-n[2],w1*(xi*xi+aa*xi+bb)**2/hh,w2*(eta*eta+aa*eta+bb)**2/hh

def coeff(endpoint,T,U,Z,W1=0,W2=0):
 missing=2 if endpoint==0 else 1;m0=1-roots[missing]
 am=iv[missing,1]+iv[missing,2]+iv[missing,3];ah=iv[missing,2]+iv[missing,3]
 M=(U-ah*T)/am;m=m0+r*M;h=r*T;z=r*r*Z;w1=r**5*W1;w2=r**5*W2
 o=step(*step(m,h,z,w1,w2))
 L1=mp.log(o[3]/w1)/r**2 if W1 else mp.nan
 L2=mp.log(o[4]/w2)/r**2 if W2 else mp.nan
 return [(o[0]-m)/r**3,(o[1]-h)/r**4,(o[2]-z)/r**5,L1,L2]
if __name__=='__main__':
 for e in [0,1]:
  for T,U,Z in [(1,1,0),(1,2,0),(1,0,0),(-1,1,0),(2,1,0),(1,1,1),(1,1,2),(1,1,-1)]:
   print(e,T,U,Z,[float(q)for q in coeff(e,T,U,Z,1,1)])
''')
print('saved',p)

For a positive integer \(s\), consider the following iteration in exact real arithmetic. Let \(n\ge1\), let \(A\in\mathbb R^{n\times n}\) satisfy \(A^\top=A\) and \(v^\top Av>0\) for every nonzero \(v\in\mathbb R^n\), and choose \(b,x_0\in\mathbb R^n\). Define
\[
x_*=A^{-1}b,\qquad r_k=b-Ax_k,\qquad \|v\|_A=(v^\top Av)^{1/2}.
\]
If \(r_k=0\), terminate. Otherwise, set
\[
\mathcal K_s(A,r_k)=\operatorname{span}\{r_k,Ar_k,\ldots,A^{s-1}r_k\}
\]
and let \(x_{k+1}\) be the unique minimizer of \(\|x_*-z\|_A\) over \(z\in x_k+\mathcal K_s(A,r_k)\). Each such minimization is one restart block, so \(k\) counts blocks. For a nonterminating orbit, define
\[
y_k=\frac{r_k}{\|r_k\|_2},\qquad \|v\|_2=(v^\top v)^{1/2}.
\]
Determine the smallest positive integer \(s\) for which there exist \(n,A,b,x_0\) as above such that \(r_k\ne0\) for every \(k\ge0\), but \((y_{2k})_{k\ge0}\) does not converge in Euclidean norm. Return \(+\infty\) if no such integer exists.

The answer is 2. Agent_1 asked me specifically in Slack #318 to derive/check **common-root FOLD ENDPOINT curvature jets**, NOT generic endpoint jets. Agent_2 suggested this in #316. We use the exact common-root stationary two-cycle:
\[
p(x)=x(x-1),\qquad q(x)=x(x-4),\qquad
H_c=\frac{1863}{4096}=0.454833984375.
\]
Core eigenvalues are four real roots of
\[
P(x)=p(x)q(x)-H_c=x^4-5x^3+4x^2-H_c.
\]
They are approximately
\[
(-0.28710307,\;0.52481464,\;0.75288657,\;4.00940185).
\]
Negative-neutral additional eigenvalues satisfy \(p(\xi)q(\xi)=-H_c\), i.e. \(P(\xi)=-2H_c\). Two real such nodes:
\[
\xi_L=9/8,\qquad \xi_R\approx3.990448464998108.
\]

In global moment coordinates
\[
m=E[x],\quad h=E[x^2]-m,\quad z=E[x^3]-E[x^2],
\]
critical core stationary line is \(h=z=0\). Its endpoints are
\[
m_L=1-\lambda_3\approx0.24711342678762248,\quad
m_R=1-\lambda_2\approx0.4751853631228165.
\]
Here missing positive-neutral core coordinate is respectively eigenvalue \(\lambda_3\) (index 2 zero-based) and \(\lambda_2\) (index 1).

### Important newest development from agent_2, Slack #320, #322, #324
Agent_2 now has **exact cubic coordinates**, much cleaner than my moments, and is deriving core jets. Read these files:
- **`workspace/critical_cubic_map_agent2.py`**
- **`workspace/critical_cubic_map_agent2.pkl`**

Let common-root core
\[
P=x^4-Sx^3+Qx^2-H_c,\quad S=u+v,\quad Q=uv.
\]
Define the unique monic cubic \(f_w\) with \(f_w(\lambda_i)=w_i P'(\lambda_i)\). Parameterize
\[
f_w=(x-z)(x^2+cx+d).
\]
(The cubic moving root `z` is NOT my moment coordinate `z`! Avoid naming collision.)

Set
\[
a=-S-c,\quad
d_0(c)=\frac{c(Q-ac)}{c-a},\qquad
b_0(c)=\frac{-a(Q-ac)}{c-a}.
\]
Stationarity defect \(\delta=d-d_0(c)\). Exact fixed two-cycle surface is \(\delta=0\):
\[
H_{\rm stat}=H_c+b_0d_0
=H_c-\frac{ac(Q-ac)^2}{(c-a)^2}.
\]
Put \(c=-v-\rho\), hence \(a=-u+\rho\). Then
\[
d_0\sim v\rho,\quad b_0\sim-u\rho,\quad
H_{\rm stat}=H_c-uv\rho^2+O(\rho^3).
\]
On stationarity,
\[
T:(z,c,d)\mapsto(z,a,b_0),
\]
so moving cubic root \(z\) is fixed, and endpoints are **exactly** \(z=\lambda_2,\lambda_3\), independent of \(\rho\). Great for endpoint analysis.

Exact rational one-step core map:
\[
m=S+c-z,
\]
\[
M_2=d-cz+Sm-Q,\quad M_3=-dz+SM_2-Qm,
\]
\[
A=\frac{mM_2-M_3}{M_2-m^2},\qquad B=-M_2-Am,
\]
\[
h_{\rm mono}=H_c+Bd-z(Ad+Bc),
\]
\[
T(z,c,d)=\left(\frac{Bdz-(m+A)H_c}{h_{\rm mono}},A,B\right).
\]
Agent_2 says all exact surface identities SymPy-checked.

**Current caveat from #324:** true \(\rho\) is outer quadratic coefficient, not the approximate `h/(1-m)`. Since \(J(T^2)-I\) on stationarity points only in moving-root \(z\)-direction,
\[
c^+-c=O(\delta^2),\quad \delta^+-\delta=O(\delta^2).
\]
With \(\delta\sim\rho^3,w\sim\rho^5\), true \(\rho'\sim w+\delta^2=O(\rho^5)\), so \(\rho\sim k^{-1/4}\), not my earlier guessed \(k^{-1/3}\). Negative fitness \(O(\rho^2)\) creates fast Dulac seed growth. Agent_2 is computing exact `(delta-linear z, delta² c, delta² delta)` jets.

Agent_1 in #326 remains skeptical: approximate interior invariant
\[
\rho\approx h/(1-m),\quad J=(H_{\rm actual}-H_c)/\rho^2
\]
has \(J\) increasing on primary scale; left-right reversal apparently would require decreasing J at one side. Endpoint logarithmically huge waiting may allow higher-order true rho changes to accumulate. Need exact cubic rho map to decide.

# 2. Rigorous solved results

## s=1 complete (agent_2)
Files:
- **`workspace/s1_complete_proof_agent2.md`** full argument.
- **`workspace/s1_check_agent2.py`** exact SymPy identity plus many rational iteration checks, passes.

Diagonalize distinct eigenvalues \(\lambda_i\), spectral weights
\(w_i=\|P_i r\|^2/\|r\|^2\).
For steepest descent,
\[
T(w)_i=w_i(\lambda_i-\mu)^2/V,\quad
\mu=E\lambda,\quad V=E(\lambda-\mu)^2.
\]
Variance monotonicity:
\[
V(Tw)-V(w)=
\frac{E[((\lambda-\mu)^2-(m_3/V)(\lambda-\mu)-V)^2]}{V}\ge0.
\]
Nontermination gives \(V_0>0\). \(V_k\to L>0\). Cluster measures supported on at most 2 roots of quadratic. For each spectral pair, fixed limiting variance gives at most two possible weights; hence finite cluster set. \(T^2\) fixes all two-point measures, so \(w_{k+2}-w_k\to0\), and finite-cluster/vanishing-step lemma gives convergence each parity. Signs on limiting pair stabilize every two blocks because both polynomial products positive eventually. Thus normalized residual even subsequence converges in Euclidean norm.

## General monic norm Lyapunov (agents_2/3)
For restarted degree \(s\), monic orthogonal \(p_w\) w.r.t. \(w\); let
\(H_s(w)=E_w p_w^2\). Weights update \(Tw=w p_w^2/H_s\), independently of SPD shift/normalization.
If \(q=p_{Tw}\), monic orthogonality gives \(E_w[pq]=H_s(w)\), so
\[
H_s(Tw)-H_s(w)
=\frac{E_w[(pq-H_s(w))^2]}{H_s(w)}\ge0.
\]
Equality iff \(pq=H_s\) on support, hence \(T^2w=w\). **No nontrivial finite weight cycles of period >2**. For \(s=2\), every cluster support has at most 4 nodes. This eliminated all exact periodic counterexample searches.

At limiting four-support equality segment, fixed limiting H means \(pq=P_4+H\); p,q factorizations finite and weights form line segments. Potential nonconvergence must drift back/forth along such segment, with delicate endpoint/extra-mode effects.

# 3. My rigorously derived endpoint formulas + checker

For a stationary **3-support endpoint** of a core4 segment:
\[
p=x^2+a x+b,\quad q=x^2+c x+d,
\]
\[
pq=P_3(x)(x-\lambda_0)+H,
\]
where \(\lambda_0\) is missing positive-neutral core eigenvalue. Face perturbations in adapted moment coordinates:
\[
h=\delta E[p],\quad z=\delta E[xp].
\]
For negative-neutral extra \(\xi\), \(p(\xi)q(\xi)=-H\), its weight \(\epsilon\) produces exact first-order two-step forcing
\[
(\Delta h,\Delta z)/\epsilon=4p(\xi)(1,\xi).
\]
This is universal; proof uses orthogonality differentiated twice. Posted #213.

Energy linear form:
\[
E=(b-d)h+(a-c)z.
\]
Let \(m=E_w x,\quad m'=m+a-c\),
\[
V=-am-b-m^2,\quad V'=-cm'-d-m'^2.
\]
Fourth-root shift in 3-face quartic:
\[
\delta\tau=t_h h+t_z z,\quad
t_h=-m/V+m'/V',\quad t_z=1/V-1/V'.
\]
Negative-extra linear logfitness:
\[
\ell_\xi(h,z)=-4\left[E/H+\delta\tau/(\xi-\lambda_0)\right].
\]
Positive missing core logfitness:
\[
\ell_0=-2P'_4(\lambda_0)\delta\tau/H.
\]
Thus forcing energy component:
\(b_E=4(H+p(\xi)^2)>0\).
I initially missed an H in conversion; correct ratio:
\[
\ell_\xi=-4E/H+
\frac{2H}{P'_4(\lambda_0)(\xi-\lambda_0)}\ell_0.
\]

One-mode limiting endpoint ODE:
\[
\dot X=b\,u,\qquad \dot u=u\,\ell X,\qquad \kappa=-\ell b>0.
\]
With \(a_0=\ell X_{\rm in}>0\),
\[
\xi(t)=\ell X(t)=-a_0\tanh(a_0t/2),\quad
u(t)=a_0^2(2\kappa)^{-1}\operatorname{sech}^2(a_0t/2).
\]
Invariant \(\xi^2+2\kappa u=a_0^2\); total pulse \(\int u=2a_0/\kappa\).
Scattering:
\[
X_{\rm out}=X_{\rm in}-2\frac{\ell X_{\rm in}}{\ell b}b.
\]
Other seed \(v_j\):
\[
v_j(t)=C\exp[(\ell_jX_{\rm mid})t]\,
\cosh(a_0t/2)^{2\ell_jb/\kappa},
\quad X_{\rm mid}=X_{\rm in}+a_0b/\kappa.
\]
Incoming/outgoing exponents are \(\ell_jX_{\rm in/out}\).
**`workspace/endpoint_pulse_check_agent3.py`** uses SymPy to verify exact ODE, invariant, limits, integral. Passes. I posted #226 and #240. Shadowing must track all seed races, not just X cone.

# 4. Generic core4 flight/reflection formulas (agent_2/4)

Canonical core:
\[
p=x(x-1),\qquad q=x^2-vx+d=(x-a)(x-L).
\]
Core roots satisfy \(P=pq-H=0\). Neg extras satisfy \(pq=-H\).
Adapted \(h=M_2-m,\;z=M_3-M_2\).
Set
\[
D=d+m^2+(v-2)m-v+1,\quad
R=m(m-1)D,
\]
\[
B=d+(v-1)(m-1),
\quad g=-dmh+Bz,\quad E=-dh+(v-1)z.
\]
Leading core interior transport has invariant \(C=g/\sqrt R\).
\[
E'= C\frac{T(m)}{2\sqrt R},\quad
T=d+2m^2+(v-3)m-v+1.
\]
At endpoints \(R=H\). In \((E,Z=\sqrt H C)\) coords, left→right flight:
\[
(E,Z)\mapsto(E+JZ,Z),\quad
J=\frac1{\sqrt H}\int_{m_L}^{m_R}\frac{T(m)}{2\sqrt{R(m)}}\,dm.
\]
Reverse uses \(-J\).
Mean velocity \(-2g(R-H)/(HR)\), so left→right requires \(Z<0\), reverse \(Z>0\).
Endpoint mean values \(m_L=1-\lambda_3,\ m_R=1-\lambda_2\).

For missing \(\lambda_0=1-m_0\), neg extra \(\xi\), define
\[
k_\xi=\frac{d(\xi-1)}{(v-1)\xi-d}.
\]
Universal reflection in (E,Z):
\[
R_\xi=I+\frac{2}{k_\xi-\xi}
\binom{1}{k_\xi-\lambda_0}(\xi-\lambda_0,-1).
\]
Fitness proportional to \(-E+Z/(\xi-\lambda_0)\).
Core departure fitness sign \(P'_4(\lambda_0)Z\):
left endpoint outgoing Z<0, right outgoing Z>0.

# 5. Major numerical lessons / long transient false leads

### CRITICAL environment warning
**`np.longdouble` is only float64 in this environment.**
`np.finfo(np.longdouble)` reports 53 bits, min subnormal 5e-324. I discovered #168. Do not describe numpy “longdouble” as higher precision.

### Moment OP2 solve can be catastrophically inaccurate
Monic q:
\[
a=(m_1m_2-m_3)/(m_2-m_1^2),\quad b=-m_2-am_1.
\]
At tiny H / near-one-node odd weights, double loses digits, can make true monotone H DECREASE. We saw false 39+ oscillations. Always monitor H monotonicity or use MPFR/stable Heine.

### Underflow-free logweights
Retain logs even when softmax inactive weights underflow. Update all logs by \(2\log|q(\lambda_i)|\), normalize common max. Otherwise tiny modes can later reinvade and freezing subnormal values falsely looks convergent.

### Stable log-Heine OP2
\[
q(\lambda_i)=
\frac{\sum_{j<k}w_jw_k(\lambda_j-\lambda_k)^2
(\lambda_i-\lambda_j)(\lambda_i-\lambda_k)}
{\sum_{j<k}w_jw_k(\lambda_j-\lambda_k)^2}.
\]
For each i exclude zero terms j=i or k=i; signed logsumexp avoids dominant cancellation and underflow. I implemented fast per-mode log numerator; files containing runs:
`workspace/innerpair_HEINE_agent3.npz`,
`workspace/innerpair_HEINE_cont_agent3.npz`.
The current scratchpad no longer holds this solver; earlier own code was overwritten; can reconstruct formula quickly.

### MPFR gold standard
Agent_2 implemented fast gmpy2:
- **`workspace/mpfr_orbit_agent2.py`** 384-bit, exact quartic Newton roots, straightforward moments at high precision, 2M T² ~20 sec.
- **`workspace/mpfr_skip_agent2.py`**, `.pkl` active3 endpoint fastforward with first-order geometric forcing. Settled huge transients in seconds.
- MPFR exponent range set ±1e9, supports extremely tiny weights without log underflow.
- Agent_1 mp100 independent checks confirmed.

1. **Exact period >2**: impossible by H Lyapunov.

   - H≤16, fixed line h=z=0 in centered moments.

   - Generic perturbations drift monotonically to 3-face endpoint, no persistent oscillation found.

   - Common root fold alone did not yield oscillator.

   - Alternating has no real zeros; nested Wronskian roots in Q>0 region, not negative-neutral.

import sympy as s,pickle,time
from pathlib import Path
z,c=s.symbols('z c');S=s.Integer(5);Q=s.Integer(4);H=s.Rational(1863,4096)
def simp(x):return s.cancel(x)
class J:
 def __init__(self,a,b=0,d=0):self.t=[simp(a),simp(b),simp(d)]
 def __add__(self,o):
  if not isinstance(o,J):o=J(o)
  return J(*[self.t[k]+o.t[k]for k in range(3)])
 __radd__=__add__
 def __neg__(self):return J(*[-x for x in self.t])
 def __sub__(self,o):return self+-o if isinstance(o,J) else self+(-o)
 def __rsub__(self,o):return -self+o
 def __mul__(self,o):
  if not isinstance(o,J):o=J(o)
  return J(*[sum(self.t[j]*o.t[k-j]for j in range(k+1))for k in range(3)])
 __rmul__=__mul__
 def inv(self):
  a,b,d=self.t;return J(1/a,-b/a**2,b*b/a**3-d/a**2)
 def __truediv__(self,o):return self*(o.inv()if isinstance(o,J)else J(1/o))
 def __rtruediv__(self,o):return self.inv()*o

def f(z,c,d):
 m=S+c-z;M2=d-c*z+S*m-Q;M3=-d*z+S*M2-Q*m;A=(m*M2-M3)/(M2-m*m);B=-M2-A*m;HH=H+B*d-z*(A*d+B*c);ZZ=(B*d*z-(m+A)*H)/HH
 return ZZ,A,B
def ds(c):return c*(Q+S*c+c*c)/(S+2*c)
start=time.time();F=f(J(z),J(c),J(ds(c),1));print('first done',time.time()-start,flush=True)
G=f(*F);print('second done',time.time()-start,flush=True)
DD=G[2]-ds(G[1]);res=[G[0]-z,G[1]-c,DD]
for name,vv in zip(['zshift','cshift','delta'],res):
 print(name,[(i,s.factor(x))for i,x in enumerate(vv.t)],flush=True)
pickle.dump((z,c,res[0].t,res[1].t,DD.t),open('workspace/critical_defect_jets_agent2.pkl','wb'))
Path('workspace/critical_defect_jets_agent2.py').write_text(Path(__file__).read_text())

import sympy as s,pickle,time
z,c,d,q,h=s.symbols('z c d q h')
def simp(x):return s.cancel(x)
class J:
 def __init__(self,a,b=0,d=0):self.t=[simp(a),simp(b),simp(d)]
 def __add__(self,o):
  if not isinstance(o,J):o=J(o)
  return J(*[self.t[k]+o.t[k]for k in range(3)])
 __radd__=__add__
 def __neg__(self):return J(*[-x for x in self.t])
 def __sub__(self,o):return self+-o if isinstance(o,J) else self+(-o)
 def __rsub__(self,o):return -self+o
 def __mul__(self,o):
  if not isinstance(o,J):o=J(o)
  return J(*[sum(self.t[j]*o.t[k-j]for j in range(k+1))for k in range(3)])
 __rmul__=__mul__
 def inv(self):
  a,b,d=self.t;return J(1/a,-b/a**2,b*b/a**3-d/a**2)
 def __truediv__(self,o):return self*(o.inv()if isinstance(o,J)else J(1/o))
 def __rtruediv__(self,o):return self.inv()*o

def f(z,c,d):
 m=c-z;M2=d-c*z-q;M3=-d*z-q*m;A=(m*M2-M3)/(M2-m*m);B=-M2-A*m;HH=h+B*d-z*(A*d+B*c);ZZ=(B*d*z-(m+A)*h)/HH;return ZZ,A,B
st=time.time();F=f(J(z),J(0,1),J(d));G=f(*F);print('sec',time.time()-st)
res=[G[0]-z,G[1]-J(0,1),G[2]-d]
for name,v in zip(['zshift','cshift','dshift'],res):print(name,[s.factor(x)for x in v.t])
pickle.dump((z,d,q,h,[v.t for v in res]),open('workspace/even_cubic_jets_agent3.pkl','wb'))

import sympy as s,pickle
z,d,q,h,t,w=s.symbols('z d q h t w');x=s.symbols('x');P=x**4+q*x*x-h
M=[1,-z,d-q,-d*z+q*z]
# m=c-z,M2=d-cz-q,M3=-dz-qm at c=0
M1,M2,M3=[M[j]+w*(t**j-M[j])for j in [1,2,3]]
AA=(M1*M2-M3)/(M2-M1*M1);BB=-M2-AA*M1
Ac=s.factor(s.diff(AA,w).subs(w,0));Bc=s.factor(s.diff(BB,w).subs(w,0));p=x*x+q-d
f=(x-z)*(x*x+d)
poly0=s.rem(f*p*p,P,x);poly1=s.rem(f*2*p*(Ac*x+Bc),P,x)
H0=-d*d+q*d+h
# normalized cubic variation
f1=s.Poly(s.cancel(poly1/H0-poly0*s.Poly(poly1,x).coeff_monomial(x**3)/H0**2),x)
dz=s.factor(-f1.as_expr().subs(x,z)/(z*z+q-d));dc=s.factor(f1.coeff_monomial(x*x)+dz);dd=s.factor(f1.coeff_monomial(x)+z*dc)
e=s.Matrix([dz,dc,dd]);print('e1',[s.factor(a)for a in e],flush=True)
# J partner: general one step
c=s.symbols('c');m=c-z;mm2=d-c*z-q;mm3=-d*z-q*m;A=s.factor((m*mm2-mm3)/(mm2-m*m));B=s.factor(-mm2-A*m);H=s.factor(h+B*d-z*(A*d+B*c));Z=s.factor((B*d*z-(m+A)*h)/H)
J=s.Matrix([Z,A,B]).jacobian([z,c,d]).subs(c,0).applyfunc(s.factor)
ep=e.subs(d,q-d, simultaneous=True);Jp=J.subs(d,q-d, simultaneous=True)
e2=(Jp*e+ep*(t*t+q-d)**2/H0).applyfunc(s.factor)
print('e2 raw',[s.factor(a)for a in e2],flush=True)
# negative neutral means (t²+d)(t²+q-d)=-H0, i.e. t4+q t²-h=-2H0
# reduce numerators mod NN
NN=s.Poly((t*t+d)*(t*t+q-d)+H0,t)
def reduce(a):
 num,den=s.fraction(s.factor(a));return s.factor(s.rem(num,NN.as_expr(),t)/den)
print('e2 reduced',[reduce(a)for a in e2],flush=True)
pickle.dump((z,d,q,h,t,e,e2,J),open('workspace/even_extra_forcing_agent3.pkl','wb'))

```

import pathlib
p=pathlib.Path('workspace/even_extra_forcing_agent3.py')
src='''"""Exact symbolic s=2 cubic-map jets and negative-extra forcing for an even quartic.
All formulas are checked algebraically. This does not establish a counterexample.
"""
import sympy as s,pickle
z,d,q,h,t,w=s.symbols('z d q h t w');x=s.symbols('x');P=x**4+q*x*x-h
M=[1,-z,d-q,-d*z+q*z]
M1,M2,M3=[M[j]+w*(t**j-M[j])for j in [1,2,3]]
AA=(M1*M2-M3)/(M2-M1*M1);BB=-M2-AA*M1
Ac=s.factor(s.diff(AA,w).subs(w,0));Bc=s.factor(s.diff(BB,w).subs(w,0));p=x*x+q-d
f=(x-z)*(x*x+d)
poly0=s.rem(f*p*p,P,x);poly1=s.rem(f*2*p*(Ac*x+Bc),P,x)
H0=-d*d+q*d+h
assert s.expand(poly0-H0*(x-z)*(x*x+q-d))==0
f1=s.Poly(s.cancel(poly1/H0-poly0*s.Poly(poly1,x).coeff_monomial(x**3)/H0**2),x)
dz=s.factor(-f1.as_expr().subs(x,z)/(z*z+q-d));dc=s.factor(f1.coeff_monomial(x*x)+dz);dd=s.factor(f1.coeff_monomial(x)+z*dc)
e=s.Matrix([dz,dc,dd])
c=s.symbols('c');m=c-z;mm2=d-c*z-q;mm3=-d*z-q*m;A=s.factor((m*mm2-mm3)/(mm2-m*m));B=s.factor(-mm2-A*m);H=s.factor(h+B*d-z*(A*d+B*c));Z=s.factor((B*d*z-(m+A)*h)/H)
J=s.Matrix([Z,A,B]).jacobian([z,c,d]).subs(c,0).applyfunc(s.factor)
ep=e.subs(d,q-d, simultaneous=True);Jp=J.subs(d,q-d, simultaneous=True)
e2=(Jp*e+ep*(t*t+q-d)**2/H0).applyfunc(s.factor)
NN=s.Poly((t*t+d)*(t*t+q-d)+H0,t)
expected=s.Matrix([-2*(q-2*d)*(t+z)*(t*t+q-d)*(z**4+q*z*z-h)/((d+z*z)*(q-d+z*z)*H0),-4*(t+z)*(t*t+q-d)/(d+z*z),-4*(t*z-d)*(t*t+q-d)/(d+z*z)])
for a,b in zip(e2,expected):
 num=s.fraction(s.cancel(a-b))[0]
 assert s.rem(num,NN.as_expr(),t)==0
print('Verified even-quartic one-step identity and all three negative-extra forcing formulas.')
'''
p.write_text(src)
exec(compile(src,str(p),'exec'))

"""Rigorous interval checker for a proposed two-endpoint billiard word.
Sage AA isolates all spectral roots exactly; RIF outward-rounded interval
Riemann sums rigorously enclose the only integral. This does NOT prove
existence of an iteration orbit: a separate return-map theorem is needed.
"""
from sage.all import *

def data(a,L,H,N=20000,bits=100):
    a,L,H=map(QQ,(a,L,H)); RIF=RealIntervalField(bits)
    P=PolynomialRing(QQ,"x");x=P.gen();Q=x*(x-1)*(x-a)*(x-L)
    core=(Q-H).roots(AA,multiplicities=False)
    extra=(Q+H).roots(AA,multiplicities=False)
    assert len(core)==len(extra)==4
    v=a+L; d=a*L
    ml,mr=1-core[2],1-core[1]
    mi,ma=RIF(ml),RIF(mr); width=ma-mi
    integral=RIF(0)
    for i in range(N):
        t=RIF(QQ(i)/N,QQ(i+1)/N);m=mi+width*t
        R=m*(m-1)*(d+m*m+(v-2)*m-v+1)
        assert R.lower()>0
        T=d+2*m*m+(v-3)*m-v+1
        integral+=T/(2*R.sqrt())*width/N
    J=integral/RIF(H).sqrt()
    F=matrix(RIF,[[1,J],[0,1]]);G=matrix(RIF,[[1,-J],[0,1]])
    refs=[];fitness=[]
    for missing in [core[2],core[1]]:
        lam=RIF(missing);rr=[];ll=[]
        for xi in extra:
            xi=RIF(xi);k=RIF(d)*(xi-1)/((v-1)*xi-d)
            col=vector(RIF,[1,k-lam]);row=vector(RIF,[xi-lam,-1])
            rr.append(identity_matrix(RIF,2)+2/(k-xi)*col.column()*row.row())
            ll.append(vector(RIF,[-1,1/(xi-lam)]))
        refs.append(rr);fitness.append(ll)
    return F,G,refs,fitness

def check(a,L,H,right_word,left_word,N=20000,bits=100):
    F,G,refs,fit=data(a,L,H,N,bits);RIF=F.base_ring()
    PR=identity_matrix(RIF,2);PL=PR
    for j in right_word: PR=refs[1][j]*PR
    for j in left_word: PL=refs[0][j]*PL
    M=PL*G*PR*F
    det=(-1)**(len(right_word)+len(left_word))
    tr=M.trace();disc=tr*tr-4*det
    assert disc.lower()>0
    eig=[(tr+disc.sqrt())/2,(tr-disc.sqrt())/2]
    rho=next(r for r in eig if r.lower()>0 and r.upper()<1)
    X=vector(RIF,[M[0,1],rho-M[0,0]])
    assert not X[0].contains_zero()
    X=-X/X[0]  # E=-1
    assert X[1].upper()<0
    Y=F*X
    assert Y[0].upper()<0
    for side,word in [(1,right_word),(0,left_word)]:
        for j in word:
            assert (fit[side][j]*Y).lower()>0
            Y=refs[side][j]*Y
            assert Y[0].upper()<0
        if side==1:
            assert Y[1].lower()>0
            Y=G*Y
            assert Y[0].upper()<0
        else:assert Y[1].upper()<0
    return rho,X,M
